\documentclass[11pt,a4paper]{article}

% =============================================================
% LUMA BOM Manager quotation parameters
% The application generates quotation_variables.tex in memory and supplies
% each QF... command below.  The same default file beside this template keeps
% the document independently compilable for layout work.
% =============================================================
\input{quotation_variables.tex}
\newcommand{\ClientCompany}{\QFClientCompany}
\newcommand{\ProjectMWp}{\QFProjectMWp}
\newcommand{\ProjectLocation}{\QFProjectLocation}
\newcommand{\ClientTitle}{\QFClientTitle}
\newcommand{\ClientName}{\QFClientName}
\newcommand{\ClientAddress}{\QFClientAddress}
\newcommand{\ClientPostalCity}{\QFClientPostalCity}
\newcommand{\ClientCountry}{\QFClientCountry}
\newcommand{\QuotationNumber}{\QFQuotationNumber}
\newcommand{\QuotationVersion}{\QFQuotationVersion}
\newcommand{\QuotationDate}{\QFQuotationDate}

% Keep this true while editing the template. Change it to
% \showplaceholdersfalse before issuing a completed quotation.
\newif\ifshowplaceholders
\showplaceholdersfalse

% =============================================================
% Page, fonts, and colours measured from LUMA_ENG.pdf
% =============================================================
\usepackage[a4paper,margin=0pt,noheadfoot]{geometry}
\usepackage{fontspec}
\usepackage[sfdefault]{carlito}
\defaultfontfeatures{Ligatures=TeX}
% Preserve the Microsoft fonts during local editing and use TeX Live's
% metrically compatible Carlito when the browser compiler cannot see them.
\IfFontExistsTF{Calibri}{\setmainfont{Calibri}}{\renewcommand{\familydefault}{\sfdefault}}
\IfFontExistsTF{Calibri}{\newfontfamily\CalibriFace{Calibri}}{\let\CalibriFace\carlito}
\IfFontExistsTF{Arial}{\newfontfamily\HelveticaFace{Arial}}{\let\HelveticaFace\carlito}
\IfFontExistsTF{Arial}{\newfontfamily\ArialFace{Arial}}{\let\ArialFace\carlito}
\IfFontExistsTF{Courier New}{\newfontfamily\CourierFace{Courier New}}{\newcommand{\CourierFace}{\ttfamily}}
\IfFontExistsTF{Times New Roman}{\newfontfamily\TimesFace{Times New Roman}}{\newcommand{\TimesFace}{\rmfamily}}

\usepackage{xcolor}
\definecolor{KSIorange}{RGB}{247,150,70}
\definecolor{KSIdark}{RGB}{64,64,64}
\definecolor{KSImidgray}{RGB}{128,128,128}
\definecolor{KSIgray}{RGB}{166,166,166}
\definecolor{KSIlightgray}{RGB}{198,198,198}

\usepackage{graphicx}
\usepackage{tikz}
\usetikzlibrary{calc}
\usepackage{eso-pic}
\usepackage{ragged2e}

\setlength{\parindent}{0pt}

\pagestyle{empty}
\hyphenpenalty=10000
\exhyphenpenalty=10000

% =============================================================
% Reusable page furniture
% Footer: every page. Header: page 2 onward.
% =============================================================
\newcommand{\KSIPageFurniture}{%
  \begin{tikzpicture}[remember picture,overlay]
    \node[anchor=south west,inner sep=0,outer sep=0]
      at ([xshift=-1.15bp,yshift=-3.48bp]current page.south west)
      {\includegraphics[width=600.80bp,height=68.40bp]{Picture/image8.png}};
    \ifnum\value{page}>1\relax
      \node[anchor=north west,inner sep=0,outer sep=0]
        at ([xshift=489.60bp,yshift=-6.80bp]current page.north west)
        {\includegraphics[width=91.20bp,height=45.60bp]{Picture/image7.png}};
    \fi
  \end{tikzpicture}%
}
\AddToShipoutPictureBG{\KSIPageFurniture}

% =============================================================
% Positioning helpers (coordinates are measured from page top-left)
% =============================================================
\newcommand{\PlaceImage}[5]{%
  \node[anchor=north west,inner sep=0,outer sep=0]
    at ([xshift=#1bp,yshift=-#2bp]current page.north west)
    {\includegraphics[width=#3bp,height=#4bp]{#5}};%
}

\newcommand{\PlaceText}[4][]{%
  \node[anchor=north west,inner sep=0,outer sep=0,align=left,#1]
    at ([xshift=#2bp,yshift=-#3bp]current page.north west)
    {#4};%
}

\newcommand{\HighlightRect}[4]{%
  \fill[yellow]
    ([xshift=#1bp,yshift=-#2bp]current page.north west)
    rectangle
    ([xshift=#3bp,yshift=-#4bp]current page.north west);%
}

% Exact-position helpers used by the editable source-page conversion.
% Word positions use the original PDF baseline, which preserves justified
% paragraph spacing without converting any page to a raster image.
\newcommand{\PlaceSourceWord}[6]{%
  \node[anchor=base west,inner sep=0,outer sep=0]
    at ([xshift=#1bp,yshift=-#2bp]current page.north west)
    {{#3\fontsize{#4bp}{#4bp}\selectfont\color[rgb]{#5}#6}};%
}

% Dynamic numeric values are anchored at the right edge of their table cell so
% different project quantities and prices cannot collide with adjacent columns.
\newcommand{\PlaceSourceValue}[6]{%
  \node[anchor=base east,inner sep=0,outer sep=0]
    at ([xshift=#1bp,yshift=-#2bp]current page.north west)
    {{#3\fontsize{#4bp}{#4bp}\selectfont\color[rgb]{#5}#6}};%
}

% Project configuration rows are generated by the application. Keeping them
% as complete lines lets projects with several array types remain aligned.
\newcommand{\PlaceConfigurationLine}[2]{%
  \PlaceSourceWord{80.064}{#1}{\ArialFace}{\QFConfigurationFontSize}{0.969,0.588,0.275}{•}%
  \PlaceSourceWord{98.064}{#1}{\CalibriFace}{\QFConfigurationFontSize}{0.349,0.349,0.349}{#2}%
}
\newcommand{\PlaceConfigurationSubline}[2]{%
  \PlaceSourceWord{87.624}{#1}{\CourierFace}{\QFConfigurationFontSize}{0.969,0.588,0.275}{o}%
  \PlaceSourceWord{105.620}{#1}{\CalibriFace}{\QFConfigurationFontSize}{0.349,0.349,0.349}{#2}%
}
\newcommand{\PlaceConfigurationDetailLine}[2]{%
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\ConfigurationLineY}{\QFConfigurationDetailStartY+(#1)*\QFConfigurationLineStep}%
  \PlaceConfigurationLine{\ConfigurationLineY}{#2}%
}
\newcommand{\PlaceConfigurationDetailSubline}[2]{%
  \pgfmathsetmacro{\ConfigurationLineY}{\QFConfigurationDetailStartY+(#1)*\QFConfigurationLineStep}%
  \PlaceConfigurationSubline{\ConfigurationLineY}{#2}%
}

\newcommand{\SourceFillRect}[7]{%
  \fill[fill={rgb,1:red,#5;green,#6;blue,#7}]
    ([xshift=#1bp,yshift=-#2bp]current page.north west)
    rectangle
    ([xshift=#3bp,yshift=-#4bp]current page.north west);%
}

% =============================================================
% LUMA introduction copy
% =============================================================
\newcommand{\JustifiedBodyLine}[1]{%
  \begingroup\hbadness=10000\noindent\makebox[\linewidth][s]{#1}\par\endgroup}
\newcommand{\NaturalBodyLine}[1]{%
  \noindent#1\par}
\newcommand{\NaturalBodyLineTo}[2]{%
  \begingroup\hbadness=10000\noindent\makebox[#1][s]{#2}\par\endgroup}

\newcommand{\LumaIntroduction}{%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{Our structure leverages our extensive experience with dual-axis tracking systems and,}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{combined with the simplicity of the 0° azimuth tracker concept, provides the best solution}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{in terms of energy yield and production while achieving the lowest possible CAPEX,}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{without compromising the exceptionally high standards of robustness and quality. At the}%
  \NaturalBodyLineTo{330.616bp}{same time, it drastically reduces OPEX thanks to its outstanding reliability.}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{The system can accommodate up to 56 photovoltaic modules with a form factor}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{exceeding one meter in width. The modules are driven as two arrays by a single SolTrk}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{electronic controller. This unique KSI tracker design allows our structure to invert the}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{module surface (optional) to prevent the accumulation of dirt, dust, snow, or other}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{deposits on the glass overnight, or to position the modules perpendicular to the ground}%
  \NaturalBodyLineTo{165.566bp}{to facilitate maintenance operations.}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{The SolTrk electronic controller is one of the key factors behind the success of the VEON}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{tracker. This robust and highly reliable control board has been continuously tested since}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{the beginning of our tracker production, and today more than 26,000 units are operating}%
  \JustifiedBodyLine{worldwide. We now have a 20-year track record demonstrating that it performs flawlessly}%
  \NaturalBodyLineTo{243.896bp}{even under the most demanding operating conditions.}%
  \vspace{15.40bp}%
  \NaturalBodyLineTo{338.228bp}{These are the reasons behind LUMA’s success and its exceptional reliability.}%
}
